- **tdigest.add**: Adds one or more observations to a t-digest sketch ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.add.md) · ★80)
- **tdigest.byrank**: Returns, for each input rank, a floating-point estimation of the value with that rank ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.byrank.md) · ★80)
- **tdigest.byrevrank**: Returns, for each input reverse rank, an estimation of the floating-point value with that reverse rank ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.byrevrank.md) · ★80)
- **tdigest.cdf**: Returns, for each input value, an estimation of the floating-point fraction of (observations smaller than the given value + half the observations equal to the given value) ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.cdf.md) · ★80)
- **tdigest.create**: Allocates memory and initializes a new t-digest sketch ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.create.md) · ★80)
- **tdigest.info**: Returns information and statistics about a t-digest sketch ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.info.md) · ★80)
- **tdigest.max**: Returns the maximum observation value from a t-digest sketch ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.max.md) · ★80)
- **tdigest.merge**: Merges multiple t-digest sketches into a single sketch ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.merge.md) · ★80)
- **tdigest.min**: Returns the minimum observation value from a t-digest sketch ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.min.md) · ★80)
- **tdigest.quantile**: Returns, for each input fraction, a floating-point estimation of the value that is smaller than the given fraction of observations ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.quantile.md) · ★80)
- **tdigest.rank**: Returns, for each floating-point input value, the estimated rank of the value (the number of observations in the sketch that are smaller than the value + half the number of observations that are equal to the value) ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.rank.md) · ★80)
- **tdigest.reset**: Resets a t-digest sketch (empties the sketch and re-initializes it). ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.reset.md) · ★80)
- **tdigest.revrank**: Returns, for each floating-point input value, the estimated reverse rank of the value (the number of observations in the sketch that are larger than the value + half the number of observations that are equal to the value) ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.revrank.md) · ★80)
- **tdigest.trimmed_mean**: Returns an estimation of the mean value from the sketch, excluding observation values outside the low and high cutoff quantiles ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/tdigest.trimmed_mean.md) · ★80)

- **ts.add**: Append a sample to a time series ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.add.md) · ★80)
- **ts.alter**: Update the retention, chunk size, duplicate policy, and labels of an existing time series ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.alter.md) · ★80)
- **ts.create**: Create a new time series ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.create.md) · ★80)
- **ts.createrule**: Create a compaction rule. ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.createrule.md) · ★80)
- **ts.decrby**: Decrease the value of the sample with the maximum existing timestamp, or create a new sample with a value equal to the value of the sample with the maximum existing timestamp with a given decrement ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.decrby.md) · ★80)
- **ts.del**: Delete all samples between two timestamps for a given time series ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.del.md) · ★80)
- **ts.deleterule**: Delete a compaction rule ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.deleterule.md) · ★80)
- **ts.get**: Get the sample with the highest timestamp from a given time series ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.get.md) · ★80)
- **ts.incrby**: Increase the value of the sample with the maximum existing timestamp, or create a new sample with a value equal to the value of the sample with the maximum existing timestamp with a given increment ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.incrby.md) · ★80)
- **ts.info**: Returns information and statistics for a time series ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.info.md) · ★80)
- **ts.madd**: Append new samples to one or more time series ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.madd.md) · ★80)
- **ts.mget**: Get the sample with the highest timestamp from each time series matching a specific filter ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.mget.md) · ★80)
- **ts.mrange**: Query a range across multiple time series by filters in forward direction ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.mrange.md) · ★80)
- **ts.mrevrange**: Query a range across multiple time-series by filters in reverse direction ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.mrevrange.md) · ★80)
- **ts.nrange**: Query a range across multiple time series in forward direction, returning the results grouped by timestamp ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.nrange.md) · ★80)
- **ts.nrevrange**: Query a range across multiple time series in reverse direction, returning the results grouped by timestamp ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.nrevrange.md) · ★80)
- **ts.queryindex**: Get all time series keys matching a filter list ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.queryindex.md) · ★80)
- **ts.querylabels**: Get all label names, or all values of a given label, for time series matching a filter list, or all series ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.querylabels.md) · ★80)
- **ts.range**: Query a range in forward direction ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.range.md) · ★80)
- **ts.read**: Read: return up to max_count samples with timestamp &gt;= timestamp. With BLOCK, waits up to milliseconds ms until at least min_count qualifying samples exist ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.read.md) · ★80)
- **ts.revrange**: Query a range in reverse direction ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ts.revrange.md) · ★80)
